							task.wait(1)

							if roughSea == 0 then
								roughSea = 7000
							else
								roughSea = 0
							end

							Instance.new("IntValue", v1835).Name = "Ignored"
							task.wait(0.5)
						end

						getgenv().RoughSea = Settings["Teleport Boat Other CFrame if Rough Sea"] and roughSea or 0

						if game.PlaceId == getgenv().CheckPlaceId then
							cFrame = t107[Settings["Select Zone"]] * CFrame.new(0, v1832.WorldPivot.Y, 0 + RoughSea)
						end

						if (v1832.VehicleSeat.Position - cFrame.Position).Magnitude > 200 then
							local cFrame2 = CFrame.new(cFrame.Position.X, v1832.WorldPivot.Y, cFrame.Position.Z)

							if LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
								v1820(v1832, cFrame2, 350)
							else
								toTarget(v1832.VehicleSeat.CFrame)
							end
						else
							if Settings["Auto Repair Ur Ship"] and LocalPlayer_2.PlayerGui.Main.BottomHUDList.ShipHealthBar.Visible then
								local v1837 = string.split(string.gsub(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.BottomHUDList.ShipHealthBar.TextLabel.Text, "Ship ", ""), "/")

								if tonumber(v1837[1]) < tonumber(v1837[2]) then
									if not (LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v1832.PrimaryPart.Position) < 20) then
										toTarget(v1832.PrimaryPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 0))
									elseif LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
										toTarget(v1832.PrimaryPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 0))
									elseif not LocalPlayer_2.Character:FindFirstChild("_RepairHammer") then
										game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.SubclassNetwork.UseSubclass:InvokeServer(unpack({ {
											Action = "RequestHammer"
										} }))
										task.wait(3)
									elseif LocalPlayer_2.Character._RepairHammer:FindFirstChild("M1UP") then
										LocalPlayer_2.Character._RepairHammer.M1UP:Destroy()
									elseif not LocalPlayer_2.Character._RepairHammer:GetAttribute("Repairing") then
										LocalPlayer_2.Character._RepairHammer.M1Down:FireServer("Default")
										task.wait(0.5)
									end

									return
								end
							end

							if not p435 then
								if not LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
									toTarget(v1832.VehicleSeat.CFrame)
								end

								local cFrame2 = CFrame.new(cFrame.Position.X, v1832.WorldPivot.Y, cFrame.Position.Z)

							toTarget(cFrame)

							if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Data.LastSpawnPoint.Value ~= "Tiki2" then

						if selectBoat == "Brigade" or selectBoat == "GrandBrigade" then
							selectBoat = "Pirate" .. Settings["Select Boat"]

			DetectSeaEvents = function(p436)
				if Settings["Select Sea Events"].SeaBeast or p436 then
					local next_ = next
					local children, v1844 = game:GetService("Workspace").SeaBeasts:GetChildren()

					for _, v1 in next_, children, v1844 do
						if v1.Name ~= "SeaBeast1" or not v1:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or not v1:FindFirstChild("HealthBBG") then
							continue
						end

						local v1847 = v1.HealthBBG.Frame.TextLabel.Text:gsub("/%d+,%d+", "")
						local Text = v1.HealthBBG.Frame.TextLabel.Text
						local v1849

						if string.find(v1847, ",") then
							v1849 = Text:gsub("%d+,%d+/", "")
						else
							v1849 = Text:gsub("%d+/", "")
						end

						local v1850 = v1849:gsub(",", "")

						if tonumber(v1850) >= 90000 and LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v1.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 2000 then
							return v1
						end
					end
				end

				if Settings["Select Sea Events"].Terrorshark or p436 then
					local Terrorshark = CheckNameBoss("Terrorshark")

					if Terrorshark and LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(Terrorshark.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 2000 then
						return Terrorshark
					end
				end

				if Settings["Select Sea Events"].Ship or p436 then
					local next_ = next
					local children, v1854 = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()

					for _, v1 in next_, children, v1854 do
						if v1:FindFirstChild("Engine") and v1:FindFirstChild("Health") and v1.Health.Value > 0 and LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v1.Engine.Position) < 2000 then
							if not Settings["Select Sea Events"]["Only Farm Ship Brigade"] then
								return v1
							end

							if table.find(t91, v1.Name) then
								return v1
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if Settings["Select Sea Events"].Shark or p436 then
					local v1857 = DetectMob(t92)

					if v1857 and LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v1857.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 2000 then
						return v1857
					end
				end

				if Settings["Select Sea Events"].Piranha or p436 then
					local Piranha = DetectMob("Piranha")

					if Piranha then
						if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(Piranha.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 2000 then
							return Piranha
						end
					end
				end

				return false
			end
			UseSkillGun = function()
				local Gun = NameWeapon("Gun", true) or false
				local Skills = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Skills

				if Gun and not Skills:FindFirstChild(Gun.Name) then
					equiptool(Gun.Name)

					return
				end

				local v1861

				if Gun and CheckCDSkillTransformation(Gun, Settings["Select Skills " .. Gun.ToolTip]) then
					local v1862

					v1861, v1862 = CheckCDSkillTransformation(Gun, Settings["Select Skills " .. Gun.ToolTip])
				end

				if v1861 then
					local Name2 = v1861.Parent.Name

					equiptool(Name2)

					if LocalPlayer_2.Character:FindFirstChild(Name2) then
						task.wait(0.2)
						game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, v1861.Name, false, game)

						if Settings["Use skill fast dont hold"] then
							task.wait(0.05)
						else
							task.wait(tonumber(holdskill))
						end

						game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, v1861.Name, false, game)
					end
				end
			end

			AutoSeabeast = function()
				if not StackFarmOther then
					return
				end

				local v1883 = DetectSeaEvents()

				if v1883 then
					v1821()

					if v1883.Name == "Terrorshark" then
						getgenv().PathTerrorshark = v1883
					end

					getgenv().PathSpinBoat = v1883

					repeat
						task.wait()
						TeleportSeaEvents(v1883)

						if not v1883:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
							local HumanoidRootPart = v1883:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or v1883:FindFirstChild("Engine")

							if HumanoidRootPart then
								if v1883.Name == "SeaBeast1" then
									getgenv().PathSeaBeast = v1883
									getgenv().AimPos = CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 40, HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z)

			AutoSpawnKitsune = function()
				local ClockTime = game.Lighting.ClockTime
				local v1902 = checkboat()

				if Settings["Hop Server Kitsune Island"] then
					local v1903 = CheckMoon()

					if not (v1903 == "Full Moon" and math.floor(18 - ClockTime) <= 5 and math.floor(18 - ClockTime) >= 0 or v1903 == "Next Night" or v1903 == "Full Moon" and ClockTime <= 5 and math.floor(5 - ClockTime) >= 11) then
						HopServer()

						return
					end
				end

				if not v1902 then
					local cFrame = CFrame.new(-13.488054275512695, 10.311711311340332, 2927.692)

					if game.PlaceId == getgenv().CheckPlaceId then
						cFrame = CFrame.new(-16204.0810546875, 9.0863618850708, 479.2259521484375)
					end

					if (cFrame.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 8 then
						toTarget(cFrame)
					else
						local selectBoat = Settings["Select Boat"]

						if selectBoat and (selectBoat == "Brigade" or selectBoat == "GrandBrigade") then
							selectBoat = "Pirate" .. selectBoat
						end

						game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", selectBoat)
						task.wait(3)
					end

					return
				end

				NoclipBoat(v1902)

				local v1906 = CFrame.new(-32975.9921875, v1902.WorldPivot.Y, 25963.7109375) * CFrame.new(0, 0, 1000)

				if CheckMoon() ~= "Full Moon" or not (math.floor(18 - ClockTime) <= 0) then
					if (v1902.VehicleSeat.Position - v1906.Position).Magnitude > 200 then
						manageTween(v1902.VehicleSeat, v1906, 350, "TweenBoat")
					end

					toTarget(v1906 * CFrame.new(0, 2000, 0))
				elseif not ((v1902.VehicleSeat.Position - v1906.Position).Magnitude > 200) then
					if not LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
						toTarget(v1902.VehicleSeat.CFrame)
					end
				elseif LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
					manageTween(v1902.VehicleSeat, v1906, 350, "TweenBoat")
				else
					toTarget(v1902.VehicleSeat.CFrame)
				end
			end

			DetectRockNear = function(p453)
				local Position = p453.VehicleSeat.Position
				local attribute = p453:GetAttribute("Size")
				local v1925 = workspace:FindPartsInRegion3(Region3.new(Position - attribute / 2, Position + attribute / 2), nil, math.huge)

				if #v1925 > 0 then
					for _ in pairs(v1925) do

			DetectSeaEventDodge = function(p456)
				if p456 then
					local next_2 = next
					local children2, v1930 = game:GetService("Workspace").SeaBeasts:GetChildren()

					for _, v1 in next_2, children2, v1930 do
						if v1.Name ~= "SeaBeast1" or not v1:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or not v1:FindFirstChild("HealthBBG") then
							continue
						end

						local v1933 = v1.HealthBBG.Frame.TextLabel.Text:gsub("/%d+,%d+", "")
						local Text = v1.HealthBBG.Frame.TextLabel.Text
						local v1935

						if string.find(v1933, ",") then
							v1935 = Text:gsub("%d+,%d+/", "")
						else
							v1935 = Text:gsub("%d+/", "")
						end

						local v1936 = v1935:gsub(",", "")

						if tonumber(v1936) >= 90000 and LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v1.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 2000 then
							return v1
						end
					end
				end

				if p456 then
					local Terrorshark = CheckNameBoss("Terrorshark")

					if Terrorshark and LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(Terrorshark.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 2000 then
						return Terrorshark
					end
				end

				if p456 then
					local next_2 = next
					local children2, v1940 = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()

					for _, v1 in next_2, children2, v1940 do
						if v1:FindFirstChild("Engine") and v1:FindFirstChild("Health") and v1.Health.Value > 0 and LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v1.Engine.Position) < 2000 then
							return v1
						end
					end
				end

				return false
			end
			AutoFindLeviathan = function()

					if LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
						getgenv().DesIdk2 = false
					end

					return
				end

				local v1943 = checkboatFind()

				if game.workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Frozen Dimension") then
					if getgenv().TweenBoat then
						getgenv().TweenBoat:Pause()
						getgenv().TweenBoat:Cancel()

					if getgenv().TweenBoatBack then
						getgenv().TweenBoatBack:Pause()
						getgenv().TweenBoatBack:Cancel()

					lib.CreateNoti({
						Title = "Banana Cat Hub",

				else
					getgenv().RespawnLeviathan = true

							if (cFrame.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 1000 then
								if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Data.LastSpawnPoint.Value == "Tiki" then
									LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0

									return
								end
							end

							toTarget(cFrame)

							game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", "Beast Hunter")
							wait(3)
						end

						getgenv().noclip = false

						local v1947 = CFrame.new(-118834.515625, 160, -78.950584411621094) * CFrame.new(0, 0, 99999999)

							elseif DistanceFindLeviathan() <= 4800 then
							end
						end

						local MaxForce = v1943.VehicleSeat.BodyVelocity.MaxForce

									getgenv().TweenBoat:Cancel()

			DetectLeviathan = function(p464, p465)
				local children2, v1989 = p464:GetChildren()

				for _, v1 in next, children2, v1989 do
					if v1.Name == "Leviathan Tail" and v1:GetAttribute("HealthEnabled") and v1.Health.Value > 0 then
						return v1
					end
				end

				local children3, v1993 = p464:GetChildren()

				for _, v1 in next, children3, v1993 do
					if v1.Name == "Leviathan" and not v1:GetAttribute("Armored") and v1.Health.Value > 0 then
						return v1
					end
				end

				if p465 then
					local next_2 = next
					local children4, v1998 = p464:GetChildren()

					for _, v1 in next_2, children4, v1998 do
						if v1.Name == "Leviathan Segment" and v1:GetAttribute("SegmentId") == p465 and v1.Health.Value > 0 then
							return v1
						end
					end
				end

							return v1

					end

			AutoAttackLeviathan = function()
				local v2007 = MultiSegmentLeviathan(game.workspace.SeaBeasts, 2) or MultiSegmentLeviathan(game.workspace.SeaBeasts, 3) or MultiSegmentLeviathan(game.workspace.SeaBeasts, 4)

				if Settings["Attack Multi Segments Leviathan"] then

					if v2007 then
						repeat
							task.wait()

							if not v2007:FindFirstChild("Tinhdamage") then
								Instance.new("IntValue", v2007).Name = "Tinhdamage"
							end

							if v2007:FindFirstChild("Tinhdamage") and v2007.Tinhdamage.Value < valueDamageMultiSegments then
								if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.DmgCounter.Visible and u1890 then

									n46 = n45
									u1890 = false
									task.wait(0.1)

							end

							if v2007.Name == "Leviathan" then
								getgenv().AimPos = v2007.Hitbox11.CFrame
								spawn(function()
									local cFrame = CFrame.new(v2007.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 140, v2007.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z)

									toTarget(cFrame)
								end)
							else
								getgenv().AimPos = v2007.Hitbox11.CFrame
								spawn(function()
									local cFrame = CFrame.new(v2007.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 142, v2007.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z)

								end)

							if Settings["Use Click M1 Fruit Leviathan"] then

								if LocalPlayer_2.Character:FindFirstChild(v2011) then

									UseSkillGun()
								end
							elseif LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2007.RootPart.Position) < 400 then
								AutoUseSkillSeabeast()
							end
						until not v2007.Parent or v2007.Health.Value == 0 or not Settings["Auto Attack Leviathan"] or v2007:FindFirstChild("Tinhdamage") and valueDamageMultiSegments <= v2007.Tinhdamage.Value

						return
					end
				end

				local v2012 = DetectLeviathan(game.workspace.SeaBeasts, 2) or DetectLeviathan(game.workspace.SeaBeasts, 3) or DetectLeviathan(game.workspace.SeaBeasts, 4) or DetectLeviathan(game.workspace.SeaBeasts)

							spawn(function()
								local cFrame = CFrame.new(v2012.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 140, v2012.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z)

								toTarget(cFrame)
							end)
						else
							getgenv().AimPos = v2012.Hitbox11.CFrame
							spawn(function()
								local cFrame = CFrame.new(v2012.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 142, v2012.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z)

								toTarget(cFrame)
							end)
						end

							equiptool(NameWeapon("Blox Fruit"))

							local v2015 = NameWeapon("Blox Fruit")

							if LocalPlayer_2.Character:FindFirstChild(v2015) then
								if LocalPlayer_2.Character[v2015]:FindFirstChild("LeftClickRemote") then
									getgenv().UseFruitM1(v2012, true)
								end
							end
						elseif Settings["Use Click M1 Skull Guitar Leviathan"] then
							equiptool(NameWeapon("Gun"))
							SpamGunSkullGuitar(v2012.Hitbox11)

							if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2012.Hitbox11.Position) < 400 then
								UseSkillGun()
							end
						elseif LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2012.Hitbox11.Position) < 400 then

				local Unit2 = ((p469 - Position) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)).Unit
				local v2021 = math.acos(math.clamp(Unit:Dot(Unit2), -1, 1))

					CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

					if p491.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") and u2091 and u2091:FindFirstChild(v2083) then
						local Humanoid = p491.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
						local v2103 = u2091:FindFirstChild(v2083)

						v2103.MaxForce = vector33

						if not p492 then
							Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
						end

						v2103.Velocity = vector3

						local moveVector = ControlModule:GetMoveVector()

						if moveVector.X > 0 then
							v2103.Velocity += CurrentCamera.CFrame.RightVector * (moveVector.X * ((p492 and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed) * 50))
						end

						if moveVector.X < 0 then
							v2103.Velocity += CurrentCamera.CFrame.RightVector * (moveVector.X * ((p492 and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed) * 50))
						end

						if moveVector.Z > 0 then
							v2103.Velocity -= CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * (moveVector.Z * ((p492 and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed) * 50))
						end

						if moveVector.Z < 0 then
							v2103.Velocity -= CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * (moveVector.Z * ((p492 and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed) * 50))
						end

						TextButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
							v2103.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -20, 0)
						end)
						TextButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
							v2103.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)
						end)
					end
				end)
			end

			BoatSettingSection.CreateToggle({
				Title = "Fly Boat",
				Desc = nil,
				Default = Settings["Fly Boat"] or false
			}, function(p493)
				if p493 then
					spawn(function()
						while Settings["Fly Boat"] and wait(0.1) do
							pcall(function()
								if LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
									if v2073 then
										v2090(LocalPlayer_2, true)
									else
										NOFLY()
										wait()
										sFLY(true)
									end

									while true do
										wait()

										if Settings["Fly Boat"] then
											if not LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
												break
											end
										else
											break
										end
									end

									if v2073 then
										v2087(LocalPlayer_2)
									else
										NOFLY()
									end
								end
							end)
						end
					end)
				end

				SaveSettings("Fly Boat", p493)
			end)
		end

		checkSpeedboat = function()
			local children, v2106 = game:GetService("Workspace").Boats:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2106 do
				if v1:IsA("Model") and v1:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") and v1.VehicleSeat.MaxSpeed + 1 < tonumber(Settings["Value Speed Boat"]) and v1:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v1.Humanoid.Value > 0 then
					return v1
				end
			end

			return false
		end
		ChangeSpeedBoat = function()
			local v2109 = checkSpeedboat()

			if v2109 then
				v2109.VehicleSeat.MaxSpeed = Settings["Value Speed Boat"] or 200
				v2109.VehicleSeat.TurnSpeed = Settings["Value Speed Boat"] or 200
			end
		end

		DetectGearUp = function()
			local Buttons = require(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.TempleGui.LocalScriptTemple.Buttons)

			Buttons.Gear1.GearType = "Default"
			Buttons.Gear4.GearType = "Default"
			Buttons.Gear5.GearType = "Default"
			Buttons.Gear2.GearType = "Alpha"
			Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = false
			Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = false
			l_Data_1 = game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "Check")
			v33 = l_Data_1.Race
			v11 = l_Data_1.HadPoint == true
			v10 = l_Data_1.RaceLevel >= 2
			v12 = l_Data_1.RaceDetails
			Buttons.Gear2.GearType = l_Data_1.RaceDetails.Gears[1] == "A" and "Alpha" or l_Data_1.RaceDetails.Gears[1] == "B" and "Omega" or "Blank"
			Buttons.Gear3.GearType = l_Data_1.RaceDetails.Gears[2] == "A" and "Alpha" or l_Data_1.RaceDetails.Gears[2] == "B" and "Omega" or "Blank"
			Buttons.Gear4.GearType = l_Data_1.RaceDetails.Gears[3] == "A" and "Alpha" or l_Data_1.RaceDetails.Gears[3] == "B" and "Omega" or "Blank"

			local Gear2 = Buttons.Gear2
			local unlocked = false

			if l_Data_1.RaceDetails.A + l_Data_1.RaceDetails.B >= 0 then
				unlocked = v10
			end

			Gear2.Unlocked = unlocked

			local Gear3 = Buttons.Gear3
			local unlocked2 = false

			if l_Data_1.RaceDetails.A + l_Data_1.RaceDetails.B >= 1 then
				unlocked2 = v10
			end

			Gear3.Unlocked = unlocked2

			local Gear4 = Buttons.Gear4
			local unlocked3 = false

			if l_Data_1.RaceDetails.A + l_Data_1.RaceDetails.B >= 2 then
				unlocked3 = v10
			end

			Gear4.Unlocked = unlocked3
			Buttons.Gear5.CanSelect = false
			Buttons.Gear5.Unlocked = false

			if l_Data_1.RaceDetails.C >= 1 then
				Buttons.Gear5.Unlocked = true
			end

			Buttons.Gear1.Unlocked = true

			if v10 then
				Buttons.Gear1.CanSelect = false
				Buttons.Gear1.GearType = "Default"
			else
				Buttons.Gear1.CanSelect = true
				Buttons.Gear1.GearType = "Blank"
				v11 = true
			end

			if v11 then
				local Gear22 = Buttons.Gear2
				local canSelect = false

				if l_Data_1.RaceDetails.A + l_Data_1.RaceDetails.B == 0 then
					canSelect = v10
				end

				Gear22.CanSelect = canSelect

				local Gear32 = Buttons.Gear3
				local canSelect2 = false

				if l_Data_1.RaceDetails.A + l_Data_1.RaceDetails.B == 1 then
					canSelect2 = v10
				end

				Gear32.CanSelect = canSelect2

				local Gear42 = Buttons.Gear4
				local canSelect3 = false

				if l_Data_1.RaceDetails.A + l_Data_1.RaceDetails.B >= 2 then
					canSelect3 = v10
				end

				Gear42.CanSelect = canSelect3

				if l_Data_1.RaceDetails.A + l_Data_1.RaceDetails.B >= 3 then
					Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = true
					Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = true
					Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = true

					if Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Omega" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Alpha" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Omega" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
						Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Omega" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
						Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Alpha" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
						Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Alpha" then
						if Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Alpha" then
							Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
							Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = false
						end
					end
				end
			else
				Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = false
				Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = false
				Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
			end

			for k, v1 in next, Buttons, nil do
				if v1.CanSelect then
					return k
				end
			end
		end
		ChooseGearV4 = function()
			local response = game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "Check")

			if response and response.HadPoint and DetectGearUp() then
				game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "SpendPoint", DetectGearUp(), "Omega")
				game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "SpendPoint", DetectGearUp(), "Alpha")
			end
		end

				end
			end
		end

		local t120 = {
			"V2InProgress",
			"V3InProgress",
			"V2TurnInReady",
			"V3TurnInReady"
		}

		AutoUpgradeRaceDraco = function()
			if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value ~= "Draco" then
				lib.CreateNoti({
					Title = "Banana Cat Hub",
					Desc = "Change Race Draco plz",
					ShowTime = 5
				})
				wait(5)

				return
			end

			if DetectItemPlr("Primordial Reign") then
				lib.CreateNoti({
					Title = "Banana Cat Hub",
					Desc = "Done V3 Draco",
					ShowTime = 5
				})
				wait(5)

				return
			end

			local DragonWizard = workspace.NPCs:FindFirstChild("Dragon Wizard") or game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").NPCs:FindFirstChild("Dragon Wizard") or NPCManager.getNPCsByName("Dragon Wizard")[1]._modelState._instance

			if getgenv().QuestDraco and (not getgenv().QuestDraco or table.find(t120, getgenv().QuestDraco.AvailableVQuest)) then
				if getgenv().QuestDraco.AvailableVQuest == "V2TurnInReady" then
					game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/InteractDragonQuest"]:InvokeServer({
						NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
						Command = "Ascension",
						Action = "Complete"
					})
					getgenv().QuestDraco = nil
				elseif getgenv().QuestDraco.AvailableVQuest == "V3TurnInReady" then
					game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/InteractDragonQuest"]:InvokeServer({
						NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
						Command = "Ascension",
						Action = "Complete"
					})
					getgenv().QuestDraco = nil
				elseif getgenv().QuestDraco.AvailableVQuest ~= "V2InProgress" then
					if getgenv().QuestDraco.AvailableVQuest == "V3InProgress" then
						SaveSettings("V3InProgress", true)

						if getgenv().KilledTerroshark then
							if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(DragonWizard.HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 8 then
								toTarget(DragonWizard.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 4, 4))
							else
								game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/InteractDragonQuest"]:InvokeServer({
									NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
									Command = "Ascension",
									Action = "Complete"
								})
								getgenv().QuestDraco = nil
								getgenv().KilledTerroshark = false
							end
						else
							local Terrorshark = CheckNameBoss("Terrorshark")
							local v2133 = checkboat()

							if Terrorshark then
								while true do
									task.wait()
									TeleportSeaEvents(Terrorshark)

									local HumanoidRootPart = Terrorshark:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									getgenv().AimPos = CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 40, HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z)
									UsedualFlock()
									ClickM1(Terrorshark, true)

									if IsMobAlive(Terrorshark) then
										if not Settings["Auto Upgrade Race V2-V3 Draco"] then
											break
										end
									else
										break
									end
								end

								getgenv().KilledTerroshark = true
							elseif v2133 then
								local v2135 = DecectPartRoughSea()

								if v2135 then
									wait(1)

									if roughSea == 0 then
										roughSea = 7000
									else
										roughSea = 0
									end

									Instance.new("IntValue", v2135).Name = "Ignored"
									wait(0.5)
								end

								getgenv().RoughSea = roughSea

								local v2136 = CFrame.new(-32975.9921875, v2133.WorldPivot.Y, 25963.7109375) * CFrame.new(0, v2133.WorldPivot.Y, 0 + RoughSea)

								if LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
									manageTween(v2133.VehicleSeat, v2136, 350, "TweenBoat")
								else
									toTarget(v2133.VehicleSeat.CFrame)
								end
							else
								local cFrame = CFrame.new(-16204.0810546875, 9.0863618850708, 479.2259521484375)

								if (cFrame.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 8 then
									toTarget(cFrame)
								else
									game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", "PirateBrigade")
								end
							end
						end
					end
				elseif CheckCountItem("Fire Flower", 5) then
					if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(DragonWizard.HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 8 then
						toTarget(DragonWizard.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 4, 4))
					else
						game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/InteractDragonQuest"]:InvokeServer({
							NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
							Command = "Ascension",
							Action = "Complete"
						})
						getgenv().QuestDraco = nil
					end
				else
					local v2138 = DetectFireFlower()

					if v2138 then
						toTarget(v2138.PrimaryPart.CFrame)

						if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2138.PrimaryPart.Position) < 8 then
							fireproximityprompt(v2138.ProximityPrompt, 1)
						end
					else
						local v2139 = DetectMob("Forest Pirate")

						if v2139 then
							repeat
								task.wait()
								sizepart(v2139)
								BringMob(v2139)
								UsedualFlock()
								ClickM1(v2139)

								if Settings["Select Weapon"] == "Blox Fruit" then
									toTarget(v2139.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-7, 20, 0))
								else
									toTarget(v2139.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(7, 20, 0))
								end
							until not IsMobAlive(v2139) or not Settings["Auto Upgrade Race V2-V3 Draco"]
						else
							local v2140 = DetectPartSpawnMob("Forest Pirate", true)

							if v2140 then
								Instance.new("IntValue", v2140).Name = "Ignored"

								while true do
									wait()
									toTarget(v2140.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0))

									if (v2140.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 100 then
										break
									elseif DetectMob("Forest Pirate") or not Settings["Auto Upgrade Race V2-V3 Draco"] or wait(1) then
										break
									end
								end
							else
								DeleteIgnoredMobSpawn()
							end
						end
					end
				end
			elseif LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(DragonWizard.HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 8 then
				toTarget(DragonWizard.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 4, 4))
			else
				getgenv().QuestDraco = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/InteractDragonQuest"]:InvokeServer({
					NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
					Command = "Speak"
				})
				wait(1)

				if getgenv().QuestDraco and getgenv().QuestDraco.AvailableVQuest == "V2" or getgenv().QuestDraco.AvailableVQuest == "V3" then
					game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/InteractDragonQuest"]:InvokeServer({
						NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
						Command = "Ascension",
						Action = "Begin"
					})
					getgenv().QuestDraco = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/InteractDragonQuest"]:InvokeServer({
						NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
						Command = "Speak"
					})
				end
			end
		end

			Title = "Auto Upgrade Race V2-V3 Draco",
			Desc = nil,

		CheckRelicChuaDat = function(p498)
			for _, descendant in pairs(p498:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and descendant.Enabled then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
		GetRelicChuaDat = function(p499)
			for k, v1 in next, p499, nil do
				if string.find(k, "RelicModel") and CheckRelicChuaDat(v1) then
					return v1, k
				end
			end
		end
		GetRelicChuanbiDat = function(p500)
			for k, v1 in next, p500, nil do
				if string.find(k, "RelicModel") then
					if v1.PrimaryPart:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition") and CheckRelicChuaDat(v1) then
						return v1, k
					end
				end
			end
		end
		CheckModelTrialDraco = function()
			local t121 = {}

			v28 = workspace:WaitForChild("Map"):WaitForChild("DracoTrial")

			for _, v1 in pairs({
				"Relic1",
				"Relic2",
				"Relic3",
				"EndRelic1",
				"EndRelic2",
				"EndRelic3",
				"Door1",
				"Door2",
				"Door3",
				"Brazier1",
				"Brazier2",
				"Brazier3",
				"Center",
				"EndPlatform",
				"TeleportOut"
			}) do
				t121[v1] = v28:FindFirstChild(v1, true)
			end

			local children, v2153 = workspace._WorldOrigin:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2153 do
				if v1:IsA("Model") and v1.Name == "Relic" then
					local MeshPart = v1:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("MeshPart")

					if MeshPart.Color == Color3.fromRGB(132, 203, 0) then
						t121.RelicModel1 = v1
					end

					if MeshPart.Color == Color3.fromRGB(232, 106, 110) then
						t121.RelicModel2 = v1
					end

					if MeshPart.Color == Color3.fromRGB(191, 153, 0) then
						t121.RelicModel3 = v1
					end
				end
			end

			return t121
		end

		ToggleAutoTrialDraco = RaceDracoSection.CreateToggle({
			Title = "Auto Trial Draco",
			Desc = nil,
			Default = Settings["Auto Trial Draco"] or false
		}, function(p501)
			if p501 then
				spawn(function()
					while Settings["Auto Trial Draco"] and task.wait(0.1) do
						local _, result = pcall(function()
							if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of Flames"].Position) <= 3000 then
								if workspace.Map.DracoTrial.TrialDoor.DoorTouch:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
									getgenv().DoneTrialDraco = true
									toTarget(workspace.Map.DracoTrial.TrialDoor.DoorTouch.CFrame)
									wait(2)

									return
								end

								if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
									local v2159 = CheckModelTrialDraco()
									local v2160, v2161 = GetRelicChuaDat(v2159)
									local v2162, v2163 = GetRelicChuanbiDat(v2159)

									if v2162 then
										local v2164 = v2159["EndRelic" .. v2163:split("RelicModel")[2]]
										local ProximityPrompt = v2164:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)

										if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2164.WorldPivot.Position) > 8 then
											toTarget(v2164.WorldPivot)
										else
											wait(2)
											fireproximityprompt(ProximityPrompt)
											wait(2)
										end
									elseif v2160 then
										local v2166 = v2159["Relic" .. v2161:split("RelicModel")[2]]
										local ProximityPrompt = v2166:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)

										if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2166.WorldPivot.Position) > 8 then
											toTarget(v2166.WorldPivot)
										else
											wait(2)
											fireproximityprompt(ProximityPrompt)
											wait(2)
										end
									end
								else
									game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.DracoTrial:InvokeServer()
									wait(3)
								end
							else
								if getgenv().DoneTrialDraco then
									lib.CreateNoti({
										Title = "Banana Cat Hub",
										Desc = "Done Trial",
										ShowTime = 5
									})
									getgenv().DoneTrialDraco = false
									ToggleAutoTrialDraco:SetStage(false)

									return
								end

								if game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") then
									if workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("TrialTeleport") then
										toTarget(workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.TrialTeleport.CFrame)
									else
										local v2168 = DetectNpc("Fossil Expert")

										if v2168 then
											toTarget(v2168.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)

											return
										end
									end
								else
									lib.CreateNoti({
										Title = "Banana Cat Hub",
										Desc = "Not have Prehistoric Island",
										ShowTime = 5
									})
									wait(5)
								end
							end
						end)

						if result then
							print(result)
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			SaveSettings("Auto Trial Draco", p501)
		end)
		DetectRockVolcano = function()
			local huge = math.huge
			local v2170
			local children, v2172 = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.VolcanoRocks:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2172 do
				if v1.Name == "Rock" then
					if v1:FindFirstChild("VFXLayer") then
						if v1.VFXLayer:FindFirstChild("Specs") and v1.VFXLayer.Specs.Enabled then
							local v2175 = LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v1.WorldPivot.Position)

							if v2175 < huge then
								huge = v2175
								v2170 = v1
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return v2170
		end
		AutoUseSkillFixLava = function()
			local Melee = Settings["Select Weapons Fix Lava"].Melee and NameWeapon("Melee", true) or false
			local Sword = Settings["Select Weapons Fix Lava"].Sword and NameWeapon("Sword", true) or false
			local bloxFruit = Settings["Select Weapons Fix Lava"]["Blox Fruit"] and NameWeapon("Blox Fruit", true) or false
			local Gun = Settings["Select Weapons Fix Lava"].Gun and NameWeapon("Gun", true) or false
			local Skills = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Skills

			if Melee and not Skills:FindFirstChild(Melee.Name) then
				equiptool(Melee.Name)

				return
			end

			if Sword and not Skills:FindFirstChild(Sword.Name) then
				equiptool(Sword.Name)

				return
			end

			if bloxFruit and not Skills:FindFirstChild(bloxFruit.Name) then
				equiptool(bloxFruit.Name)

				return
			end

			if Gun and not Skills:FindFirstChild(Gun.Name) then
				equiptool(Gun.Name)

				return
			end

			local v2181

			if Melee and CheckCDSkillTransformation(Melee, Settings["Select Skills " .. Melee.ToolTip]) then
				local v2182

				v2181, v2182 = CheckCDSkillTransformation(Melee, Settings["Select Skills " .. Melee.ToolTip])
			elseif Sword and CheckCDSkillTransformation(Sword, Settings["Select Skills " .. Sword.ToolTip]) then
				local v2183

				v2181, v2183 = CheckCDSkillTransformation(Sword, Settings["Select Skills " .. Sword.ToolTip])
			elseif Gun and CheckCDSkillTransformation(Gun, Settings["Select Skills " .. Gun.ToolTip]) then
				local v2184

				v2181, v2184 = CheckCDSkillTransformation(Gun, Settings["Select Skills " .. Gun.ToolTip])
			elseif bloxFruit and CheckCDSkillTransformation(bloxFruit, Settings["Select Skills " .. bloxFruit.ToolTip]) then
				local v2185

				v2181, v2185 = CheckCDSkillTransformation(bloxFruit, Settings["Select Skills " .. bloxFruit.ToolTip])
			end

			if v2181 then
				local Name2 = v2181.Parent.Name

				equiptool(Name2)

				if LocalPlayer_2.Character:FindFirstChild(Name2) then
					game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, v2181.Name, false, game)

					if Settings["Use skill fast dont hold"] then
						task.wait(0.05)
					else
						task.wait(tonumber(holdskill))
					end

					game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, v2181.Name, false, game)
				end
			end
		end
		DetectLava = function()
			local descendants, v2188 = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()

			for _, v1 in next, descendants, v2188 do
				if v1.Name ~= "TouchInterest" or v1.Parent.Name == "TrialTeleport" then
					continue
				end

				return true
			end
		end
		DetectGolem = function()
			for _, child in ipairs(game.workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "Lava Golem" and IsMobAlive(child) and LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position) <= 1500 then
					return child
				end
			end
		end
		DeleteLava = function()
			local children, v2194 = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.InteriorLava:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2194 do
				v1:Destroy()
			end
		end
		DetectPositionVolcano = function()
			local t122 = { workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.PrehistoricRelic.Skull.Position }
			local descendants, v2199 = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()

			for _, v1 in next, descendants, v2199 do
				if v1:IsA("MeshPart") and v1.MeshId == "rbxassetid://87519803677536" and math.floor(v1.Position.Y) == 293 then
					t122[2] = v1.Position
				end

				if v1:IsA("MeshPart") and v1.MeshId == "rbxassetid://9664674474" and math.floor(v1.Position.Y) == 234 then
					t122[3] = v1.Position
				end

				if v1:IsA("MeshPart") and v1.MeshId == "rbxassetid://14130842310" and math.floor(v1.Position.Y) == 266 then
					t122[4] = v1.Position
				end

				if v1:IsA("MeshPart") and v1.MeshId == "rbxassetid://15672470777" and math.floor(v1.Position.Y) == 86 then
					t122[5] = v1.Position
				end

				if v1:IsA("MeshPart") and v1.MeshId == "rbxassetid://5159878936" then
					if math.floor(v1.Position.Y) == 261 then
						t122[6] = v1.Position
					end
				end

				if v1:IsA("MeshPart") and v1.MeshId == "rbxassetid://138849514693209" and math.floor(v1.Position.Y) == 242 then
					t122[7] = v1.Position
				end

				if v1:IsA("MeshPart") and v1.MeshId == "rbxassetid://87519803677536" and math.floor(v1.Position.Y) == 279 then
					t122[8] = v1.Position
				end
			end

			return t122
		end
		CheckPosnearRock = function(p502, p503)
			local huge = math.huge
			local v2203
			local n48 = 0

			for k, v1 in next, p502, nil do
				local vector3 = Vector3.new(v1.X, 0, v1.Z)
				local Magnitude = (Vector3.new(p503.Position.X, 0, p503.Position.Z) - vector3).Magnitude

				if Magnitude < huge then
					huge = Magnitude
					v2203 = v1
					n48 = k
				end
			end

			return v2203, n48
		end

		local u2209 = false
		local n49 = 1
		local t123 = {

		BuyGearDracoV4 = function()
			if string.find(CheckAcientOneDracoStatus(), "Can Buy Gear") then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("UpgradeRace", "Buy", 2)

		FullyDraco = function()
			if not Settings["Auto Turn On V4"] then
				v986:SetStage(true)
			end

			if not Settings["Auto Choose Gears"] and getgenv().ToggleAutoChooseGears then
				getgenv().ToggleAutoChooseGears:SetStage(true)
			end

			if CheckAcientOneDracoStatus() == "Ready For Trial" then
				if getgenv().WaitingjoinTrial then
					wait(5)
					getgenv().WaitingjoinTrial = false
				end

				if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of Flames"].Position) <= 3000 then
					if workspace.Map.DracoTrial.TrialDoor.DoorTouch:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
						getgenv().DoneTrialDraco = true
						toTarget(workspace.Map.DracoTrial.TrialDoor.DoorTouch.CFrame)
						wait(2)

						return
					end

					if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
						local v2212 = CheckModelTrialDraco()
						local v2213, v2214 = GetRelicChuaDat(v2212)
						local v2215, v2216 = GetRelicChuanbiDat(v2212)

						if v2215 then
							local v2217 = v2212["EndRelic" .. v2216:split("RelicModel")[2]]
							local ProximityPrompt = v2217:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)

							if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2217.WorldPivot.Position) > 8 then
								toTarget(v2217.WorldPivot)
							else
								wait(2)
								fireproximityprompt(ProximityPrompt)
								wait(2)
							end
						elseif v2213 then
							local v2219 = v2212["Relic" .. v2214:split("RelicModel")[2]]
							local ProximityPrompt = v2219:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)

							if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2219.WorldPivot.Position) > 8 then
								toTarget(v2219.WorldPivot)
							else
								wait(2)
								fireproximityprompt(ProximityPrompt)
								wait(2)
							end
						end
					else
						game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.DracoTrial:InvokeServer()
						wait(3)
					end
				else
					if getgenv().DoneTrialDraco then
						wait(5)
						getgenv().DoneTrialDraco = false

						return
					end

					if game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") then
						if getgenv().turnoffnoclipBoatt then
							getgenv().turnoffnoclipBoatt = false
							TurnOffNoclipBoat(Boat)
						end

						if getgenv().RespawnVolcano and Settings["Webhook Find Prehistoric Island"] then
							getgenv().RespawnVolcano = false
							WebhookFindVolcano()
						end

						if getgenv().TweenBoat then
							getgenv().TweenBoat:Pause()
							getgenv().TweenBoat:Cancel()
						end

						if not LocalPlayer_2:GetAttribute("CurrentLocation") or LocalPlayer_2:GetAttribute("CurrentLocation") ~= "Prehistoric Island" then
							local v2221 = DetectNpc("Fossil Expert")

							if v2221 then
								toTarget(v2221.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)

								return
							end
						end

						if workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("TrialRock", true).Transparency == 1 then
							getgenv().WaitingjoinTrial = true
							toTarget(workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.TrialTeleport.CFrame)

							return
						end

						if DetectLava() then
							local next_ = next
							local descendants, v2224 = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()

							for _, v1 in next_, descendants, v2224 do
								if v1.Name == "TouchInterest" and v1.Parent.Name ~= "TrialTeleport" then
									v1:Destroy()
								end
							end
						end

						if #workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.InteriorLava:GetChildren() > 0 then
							DeleteLava()
						end

						local ok = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.PrehistoricRaidTimer.Visible

						if not ok then
							ok = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible
						end

						if ok then
							if u2209 then
								local Skull = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.PrehistoricRelic.Skull

								while true do
									task.wait()
									toTarget(Skull.Position, Skull.CFrame)

									if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(Skull.Position) <= 200 then
										break
									elseif DetectGolem() or DetectRockVolcano() then
										break
									end
								end

								u2209 = false

								return
							end

							local v2229 = DetectGolem()

							if v2229 then
								while true do
									task.wait()
									toTarget(v2229.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 7))

									if Settings["Select Method Kill Golem"] ~= "Instant Kill [ Risk and can bug no die mob ]" then
										equiptool(NameWeapon(Settings["Select Weapon Kill Golem"] or "Melee"))
										getgenv().ClickM1Volcano(v2229)
									elseif LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2229.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 50 then
										KillRaidEnemy()
									end

									if not getgenv().KillMobRaid and Settings["Kill Aura Only Raid And Volcano"] then
										getgenv().KillMobRaid = true

										local timeDelayKill = Settings["Time Delay Kill"] or 5

										v2229.Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
										delay(timeDelayKill, function()
											getgenv().KillMobRaid = false
										end)
									end

									if IsMobAlive(v2229) and Settings["Fully Trial Draco"] then
										if game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") then
											if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.PrehistoricRaidTimer.Visible then
												continue
											elseif game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
												continue
											end
										end
									end

									break
								end
							end

							local v2231 = DetectRockVolcano()

							if v2231 then
								if Settings["Fix Volcano Safe"] then
									local v2232 = DetectPositionVolcano()
									local _, v2234 = CheckPosnearRock(v2232, LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
									local v2235 = CheckPosnearRock(v2232, v2231.WorldPivot)

									if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2235) >= 400 then
										n49 = v2234 + 1

										if v2234 >= 7 then
											n49 = 1
										end

										toTarget(CFrame.new(v2232[n49]))
									else
										local v2236 = t123[math.floor(v2231.WorldPivot.Position.Y)]

										while true do
											task.wait()

											if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter((v2231.WorldPivot * v2236).Position) > 8 then
												toTarget(v2231.WorldPivot * v2236)
											end

											if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2231.WorldPivot.Position) < 100 then

												if game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") then
													if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.PrehistoricRaidTimer.Visible then
														continue
													elseif game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
														continue
													end
												end
											end

											break
										end

										if not DetectGolem() then
											u2209 = true
										end

										wait(1)
									end
								else
									local v2237 = t123[math.floor(v2231.WorldPivot.Position.Y)]

									while true do
										task.wait()

										if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter((v2231.WorldPivot * v2237).Position) > 8 then
											toTarget(v2231.WorldPivot * v2237)
										end

										if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2231.WorldPivot.Position) < 100 then
											AutoUseSkillFixLava()
										end

										getgenv().AimPos = v2231.WorldPivot
										Mouse.Hit = v2231.WorldPivot
										Mouse.Target = v2231

										if v2231.Parent and Settings["Fully Trial Draco"] and v2231.VFXLayer.Specs.Enabled and not DetectGolem() then
											if game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") then
												if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.PrehistoricRaidTimer.Visible then
													continue
												elseif game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
													continue
												end
											end
										end

										break
									end

									if not DetectGolem() then
										u2209 = true
									end
								end
							end
						else
							if workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild("ActivationPrompt") then
								if workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.ActivationPrompt:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") then
									toTarget(workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.ActivationPrompt.CFrame)

									if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.ActivationPrompt.Position) < 8 then

										wait(3)

								end

					else

							getgenv().dacoMagnet = true

							if not game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") then

									if not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then

													getgenv().TweenBoat:Pause()
													getgenv().TweenBoat:Cancel()
												end

												toTarget(v2239.VehicleSeat.CFrame)
											end
										else
											local cFrame = CFrame.new(-16204.0810546875, 9.0863618850708, 479.2259521484375)

											if (cFrame.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 8 then
												if not ((cFrame.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 1000) or LocalPlayer_2:GetAttribute("CurrentLocation") and LocalPlayer_2:GetAttribute("CurrentLocation") == "Tiki Outpost" then
													toTarget(cFrame)

													return
												end

												if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Data.LastSpawnPoint.Value ~= "Tiki" then
													if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Data.LastSpawnPoint.Value ~= "Tiki2" then
														toTarget(cFrame)

														return
													end
												end

												LocalPlayer_2.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0

												return
											else
												game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", "PirateBrigade")
												wait(3)
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						else
							if getgenv().dacoMagnet then
								local now2 = tick()

								while true do
									wait()

									if tick() - now2 >= 5 then
										break
									elseif game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") then
										break
									end
								end

								getgenv().dacoMagnet = false

								return
							end

							if not CheckCountItem("Scrap Metal", 10) then
								local t124 = {
									"Jungle Pirate",
									"Musketeer Pirate"
								}
								local v2243 = DetectMob(t124)

								if v2243 then
									repeat
										task.wait()
										sizepart(v2243)
										BringMob(v2243)
										UsedualFlock()
										ClickM1(v2243)

										if Settings["Select Weapon"] == "Blox Fruit" then
											toTarget(v2243.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-7, 20, 0))
										else
											toTarget(v2243.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(7, 20, 0))
										end
									until not IsMobAlive(v2243) or not Settings["Fully Trial Draco"]
								else
									local v2244 = t124

									if typeof(v2244) == "table" then
										if #v2244 <= #t71 then
											t71 = {}

											return
										end

										local v2245 = DetectPartSpawnMob(DetectNameTablePart(v2244))

										if v2245 then
											table.insert(t71, DetectNameTablePart(v2244))

											while true do
												wait()
												toTarget(v2245.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0))

												if (v2245.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 100 then
													break
												elseif DetectMob(v2244) or not Settings["Fully Trial Draco"] then
													break
												end
											end

											wait(1)
										end
									end
								end

								return
							end

							if not CheckCountItem("Blaze Ember", 15) then
								local DragonHunter = workspace.NPCs:FindFirstChild("Dragon Hunter") or game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").NPCs:FindFirstChild("Dragon Hunter") or NPCManager.getNPCsByName("Dragon Hunter")[1]._modelState._instance

								if getgenv().QuestHunterDragon then
									local v2247 = DetectEmberTemplate()

									if v2247 then
										Instance.new("IntValue", v2247).Name = "Ignored"

										repeat
											wait()
											toTarget(v2247.Part.CFrame)
										until not v2247.Parent

										return
									end

									if string.find(getgenv().QuestHunterDragon, "Hydra Enforcers") then
										local v2248 = DetectMob("Hydra Enforcer")

										if v2248 then
											repeat
												task.wait()
												sizepart(v2248)
												BringMob(v2248)
												UsedualFlock()
												ClickM1(v2248)

												if Settings["Select Weapon"] == "Blox Fruit" then
													toTarget(v2248.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-7, 20, 0))
												else
													toTarget(v2248.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(7, 20, 0))
												end
											until not IsMobAlive(v2248) or not Settings["Fully Trial Draco"]
										else
											local v2249 = DetectPartSpawnMob("Hydra Enforcer", true)

											if v2249 then
												Instance.new("IntValue", v2249).Name = "Ignored"

												while true do
													wait()
													toTarget(v2249.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0))

													if (v2249.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 100 then
														break
													elseif DetectMob("Hydra Enforcer") or not Settings["Fully Trial Draco"] then

		AutoMinkV2 = function()
			local v2269 = GetNearestChest()

			if v2269 then
				local now2

				while true do
					task.wait()

					if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - v2269.Position).Magnitude <= 5 then
						if now2 then
							if tick() - now2 >= 5 then
								Instance.new("IntValue", v2269).Name = "Ignored"
								wait(0.5)
							end
						else
							now2 = tick()
						end

						game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "Space", false, game)
						wait()
						game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, "Space", false, game)
						TweenManager.CancelCurrent()
					end

					toTarget(v2269.CFrame, true)

					if v2269.Parent and Settings["Auto Upgrade Race V2-V3"] and not v2269:GetAttribute("IsDisabled") then
						if not v2269:FindFirstChild("Ignored") and v2269:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
							continue
						end
					end

					break
				end
			else
				local v2271 = PathFindChest()

				if v2271 then
					toTarget(v2271.Part.CFrame)

					if (v2271.Part.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 100 or GetNearestChest() then
						Instance.new("IntValue", v2271).Name = "Ignored"
					end
				else
					for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.PlayerSpawns.Pirates:GetChildren()) do
						if child:FindFirstChild("Ignored") then
							child:FindFirstChild("Ignored"):Destroy()
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		DetectSeabeast = function()
			local children, v2275 = game:GetService("Workspace").SeaBeasts:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2275 do
				if v1.Name ~= "SeaBeast1" then
					continue
				end

				local v2278 = v1.HealthBBG.Frame.TextLabel.Text:gsub("/%d+,%d+", "")
				local Text = v1.HealthBBG.Frame.TextLabel.Text
				local v2280

				if string.find(v2278, ",") then
					v2280 = Text:gsub("%d+,%d+/", "")
				else
					v2280 = Text:gsub("%d+/", "")
				end

				local v2281 = v2280:gsub(",", "")

				if tonumber(v2281) >= 90000 then
					return v1
				end
			end

			return false
		end

								end

								wait(5)
							end

							local v2312 = DetectPlayerGhoul()

							if v2312 then
								table.insert(t126, v2312.Name)

										end

										else
											toTarget(v2312.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3))
										end

										if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v2312.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 50 then
											AutoAllSkill(true)
										end
									end)

									if tick() - now2 >= 70 then
										break
									elseif not v2312.Character or not v2312.Character.Parent or v2312.Character.Humanoid.Health == 0 or CheckSafezone(v2312.Character) or CheckPlayercantAttack(v2312.Character) or not Settings["Auto Upgrade Race V2-V3"] then
										break
									end
								end

								wait(5)
							end

				if game.workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland") then

						if child:IsA("MeshPart") and child.MeshId == "rbxassetid://10153114969" then
							return child
						end
					end
				end
			end
			getHighestPoint = function()
				if not game.workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland") then
					return nil
				end

				for _, descendant in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Map.MysticIsland:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") and descendant.MeshId == "rbxassetid://6745037796" then
						return descendant
					end
				end

				"Last Resort",

						return true
					end

				for _, v1 in next, children2, v2357 do

					if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time") then

						return
					end

					local ok = cFrame.Z + 0.2 < game:GetService("Workspace").Map["Temple of Time"].Lever.Lever.CFrame.Z

					if not ok then
						ok = not not (game:GetService("Workspace").Map["Temple of Time"].Lever.Lever.CFrame.Z < cFrame.Z - 0.2)
					end

							Title = "Banana Cat Hub",
							Desc = "Done Pull Lever",
							ShowTime = 5
						})
						wait(5)
					end
				else
					local response = game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "Check")

					if response == 1 then
						game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "Begin")

						return
					end

					if response == 2 then
						toTarget(CFrame.new(3032.780029296875, 2280.85107421875, -7325.47802734375))

							game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "Teleport")
						end

						return
					end

						game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "Continue")

						return
					end

					if game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland") and CheckClockTime() == "Night" then
						CollectBlueGear()
					elseif game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland") and CheckClockTime() ~= "Night" then
						if not getHighestPoint() then
							local v2366 = DetectNpc("Advanced Fruit Dealer")

							if v2366 then
								toTarget(v2366.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)

								return
							end
						end

						if getHighestPoint() and (getHighestPoint().CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 211.88, 0).Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
							toTarget(getHighestPoint().CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 211.88, 0))
						end
					elseif not game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland") and Settings["Hop Server [Trial Or Pull Lever]"] then
						SpecialHop("Mirage")
					end
				end

				Title = "Auto Buy Gear",
				Desc = nil,

				Title = "Auto Finish Train Quest",

				Default = Settings["Auto Finish Train Quest"] or false
			}, function(p521)
				if p521 then
					spawn(function()
						while Settings["Auto Finish Train Quest"] do
							if not task.wait() then
								break
							end

							local _, result = pcall(function()
								if Settings["Stack Train With Trial Race"] and not CheckGoTrain() then
									return
								end

								if LocalPlayer_2.Character:FindFirstChild("RaceEnergy") and LocalPlayer_2.Character.RaceEnergy.Value >= 1 then
									if LocalPlayer_2.Character:FindFirstChild("RaceTransformed") and not LocalPlayer_2.Character.RaceTransformed.Value then
										LocalPlayer_2.Backpack.Awakening.RemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack({ true }))
									end
								end

								BuyGearV4()

								local v2371 = DetectMob(t72)

								if v2371 then
									repeat
										task.wait()
										sizepart(v2371)
										BringMob(v2371)
										UsedualFlock()
										ClickM1(v2371)

										if Settings["Select Weapon"] == "Blox Fruit" then
											toTarget(v2371.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-7, 20, 0))
										else
											toTarget(v2371.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(7, 20, 0))
										end
									until not IsMobAlive(v2371) or not Settings["Auto Finish Train Quest"] or not CheckGoTrain()
								else
									local v2372 = t72

									if typeof(v2372) == "table" then
										if #v2372 <= #t71 then
											t71 = {}

											return
										end

										local v2373 = DetectPartSpawnMob(DetectNameTablePart(v2372))

										if v2373 then
											table.insert(t71, DetectNameTablePart(v2372))

											while true do
												wait()
												toTarget(v2373.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0))

												if (v2373.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 100 then
													break
												elseif DetectMob(v2372) or not Settings["Auto Finish Train Quest"] or not CheckGoTrain() then
													break
												end
											end

											wait(1)
										end
									else
										local v2374 = DetectPartSpawnMob(v2372, true)

										if v2374 then
											Instance.new("IntValue", v2374).Name = "Ignored"

											while true do
												wait()
												toTarget(v2374.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0))

												if (v2374.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 100 then
													break
												elseif DetectMob(v2372) or not Settings["Auto Finish Train Quest"] or not CheckGoTrain() then
													break
												end
											end

											wait(1)
										else
											DeleteIgnoredMobSpawn()
										end
									end
								end
							end)

							if result then
								print(result)
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				SaveSettings("Auto Finish Train Quest", p521)
			end)

		CheckMultiPlayerNearDoor = function()
			local n51 = 0
			local children, v2392 = game.Workspace.Characters:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2392 do
				local v2395 = GetOtherPlayerRaces()[v1.Name]

				if v2395 and DetectNameAbility(v1.HumanoidRootPart) then
					if (v1.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game:GetService("Workspace").Map["Temple of Time"][v2395 .. "Corridor"].Door.Door.RightDoor.Union.Position).Magnitude < 100 then
						n51 += 1
					end
				end
			end

			if n51 >= 2 then
				return true
			end
		end
		CheckMultiAccount = function()

					t132[child.Name] = child.Data.Race.Value

			end

			return t132

		TrialHuman = function()
			if game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Strength") then
				StrengthPart = game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of Strength"]

				if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - StrengthPart.Position).Magnitude <= 1000 then
					for _, child in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if IsMobAlive(child) and (child.HumanoidRootPart.Position - StrengthPart.Position).Magnitude <= 1000 then
							return child
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		TrialGhoul = function()
			if game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Carnage") then
				if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of Carnage"].Position).Magnitude <= 1000 then
					for _, child in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if IsMobAlive(child) then
							if (child.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of Carnage"].Position).Magnitude <= 1000 then
								return child
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		GetSeaBeastTrial = function()
			if not game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("FishmanTrial") then
				return
			end

			local TrialOfWater

			if game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water") then
				TrialOfWater = game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water")
			end

			if TrialOfWater then
				local next_ = next
				local children, v2413 = game:GetService("Workspace").SeaBeasts:GetChildren()

				for _, v1 in next_, children, v2413 do
					if string.find(v1.Name, "SeaBeast") and v1:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (v1.HumanoidRootPart.Position - TrialOfWater.Position).Magnitude <= 1500 and v1.Health.Value > 0 then
						return v1
					end
				end
			end
		end
		getgenv().TrialDone = false
		getgenv().KillAuraDone = false
		TeleportSeabeast2 = function(p537)
			if (Vector3.new(0, p537:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position.Y, 0) - Vector3.new(0, -60, 0)).Magnitude <= 175 then
				toTarget(p537.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 200, 50))
			else
				toTarget(CFrame.new(p537.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 140, p537.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
			end
		end
		DetectPlayerKillName = function()
			local t133 = {}
			local children, v2418 = game.Workspace.Characters:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2418 do
				if v1:IsA("Model") and v1.Name ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name and v1:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v1:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v1.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (v1.HumanoidRootPart.Position - Vector3.new(28718.068359375, 14887.5625, -60.5482177734375)).Magnitude <= 400 then
					table.insert(t133, v1.Name)
				end
			end

			return t133
		end

		getgenv().BlackListPlayerTrial = {}

			for k, v1 in next, getgenv().PlayerKillTrial, nil do

		CheckCDSkill = function(p538)
			if not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Skills:FindFirstChild(p538) then
				equiptool(p538)

				return
			end

			local children, v2424 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Skills[p538]:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2424 do
				if v1:IsA("Frame") then
					if v1.Name ~= "Template" and v1.Title.TextColor3 == Color3.new(1, 1, 1) and v1.Cooldown.Size == UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1) then
						return v1
					end

					if v1.Cooldown.Size == UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -1) then
						return v1
					end
				end
			end
		end
		VerifyNearbyTrial = function()
			local t134 = {
				"Trial of the Machine",
				"Trial of Speed",
				"Trial of Strength",
				"Trial of Water",
				"Trial of the King",
				"Trial of Carnage",
				"Trial of Flames"
			}
			local children, v2429 = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren()

			for _, v1 in next, children, v2429 do
				if table.find(t134, v1.Name) then
					if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(v1.Position) < 1500 then
						return true
					end
				end
			end
		end
		AutoTrialV4 = function()
			if Settings["Auto Finish Train Quest"] and Settings["Stack Train With Trial Race"] and CheckGoTrain() then
				return
			end

			local ClockTime = game.Lighting.ClockTime

			if (CheckMoon() == "Full Moon" and (not (ClockTime > 5) or not (ClockTime < 12)) or CheckMoon() == "Next Night") and Settings["Hop Server [Trial Or Pull Lever]"] then
				if getgenv().TurnOffHOPSVPullAndTrial then
					getgenv().TurnOffHOPSVPullAndTrial:SetStage(false)
				end

				task.wait(3)
			elseif Settings["Hop Server [Trial Or Pull Lever]"] then
				HopServer()

				return
			end

			if not (workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time") or VerifyNearbyTrial()) then
				toTarget(CFrame.new(28282.5703125, 14896.8505859375, 105.10427093505859))

				return
			end

			local ok = not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time")

			if not ok then
				ok = not (not not workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].FFABorder:FindFirstChild("Forcefield") and workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].FFABorder.Forcefield.Transparency == 1)
			end

			local Value, v2435, game_, DistanceFromCharacter, Locations, TrialOfWater, v2440, HumanoidRootPart, v2442, game_2, game_3, Union

			if ok then
				if VerifyNearbyTrial() then
					local game_4 = game

					if game_4:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
						if VerifyNearbyTrial() and not getgenv().VerifyTrial then
							getgenv().VerifyTrial = true
						end

						local game_5

						repeat
							wait()

							if VerifyNearbyTrial() then
								break
							end

							game_5 = game
						until not game_5:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible

						Value = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value

						if Value == "Human" then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								v2442 = TrialHuman()

								if v2442 then
									while true do
										task.wait()
										sizepart(v2442)

										if Settings["Select Weapon"] == "Blox Fruit" then
											toTarget(v2442.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-7, 20, 0))
										else
											toTarget(v2442.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(7, 20, 0))
										end

										ClickM1(v2442)
										UsedualFlock()

										if IsMobAlive(v2442) then
											game_2 = game

											if game_2:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
												game_3 = game

												if not ((game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game_3:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of Strength"].Position).Magnitude > 1000) then
													continue
												end
											end
										end

										break
									end
								end

								local game_6 = game

								if game_6:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
									local game_7 = game

									if not ((game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game_7:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of Strength"].Position).Magnitude > 1000) then
										continue
									end
								end

								break
							end
						elseif Value == "Skypiea" then
							while true do
								task.wait()

								local game_6 = game

								if game_6:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of the King"] then
									local game_7 = game

									if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game_7:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of the King"].CFrame.Position).Magnitude <= 1000 then
										toTarget(game:GetService("Workspace").Map.SkyTrial.Model.FinishPart.CFrame)
										task.wait(3)
									end
								end

								local game_7 = game

								if game_7:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
									local Map = workspace.Map

									if not Map:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time") or workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].FFABorder.Forcefield.Transparency ~= 0 then
										local game_8 = game

										if not (LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(game_8:GetService("Workspace").Map.SkyTrial.Model.FinishPart.Position) > 1000) then
											continue
										end
									end
								end

								break
							end
						elseif Value == "Fishman" then
							TrialOfWater = nil

							local Locations2 = game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations

							if Locations2:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water") then
								TrialOfWater = game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water")
							end

							if TrialOfWater and (TrialOfWater.Position - LocalPlayer_2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < 1500 then
								v2440 = GetSeaBeastTrial()

								while true do
									task.wait()

									if v2440 then
										HumanoidRootPart = v2440:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										getgenv().AimPos = CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 40, HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z)
										TeleportSeabeast2(v2440)

										if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 400 then
											AutoAllSkill()
										end
									end

									if v2440 and v2440.Parent and v2440.Health.Value ~= 0 then
										local game_6 = game

										if game_6:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
											local Map = workspace.Map

											if not Map:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time") or workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].FFABorder.Forcefield.Transparency ~= 0 then
												local DistanceFromCharacter2 = LocalPlayer_2.DistanceFromCharacter
												local Locations3 = game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations

												if not (DistanceFromCharacter2(LocalPlayer_2, Locations3:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water").Position) > 1000) then
													continue
												end
											end
										end
									end

									break
								end
							end
						elseif Value == "Mink" then
							while true do
								task.wait()

								local game_6 = game

								if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game_6:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations["Trial of Speed"].Position).Magnitude <= 1000 then
									toTarget(game:GetService("Workspace").StartPoint.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 0))
								end

								local game_7 = game

								if game_7:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
									local DistanceFromCharacter2 = LocalPlayer_2.DistanceFromCharacter
									local Locations2 = game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations

									if not (DistanceFromCharacter2(LocalPlayer_2, Locations2:FindFirstChild("Trial of Speed").Position) > 1000) then
										continue
									end
								end

								break
							end
						elseif Value == "Ghoul" then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								v2435 = TrialGhoul()

								if v2435 then
									while true do
										task.wait()
										sizepart(v2435)

										if Settings["Select Weapon"] == "Blox Fruit" then
											toTarget(v2435.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-7, 20, 0))
										else
											toTarget(v2435.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(7, 20, 0))
										end

										UsedualFlock()
										ClickM1(v2435)

										if IsMobAlive(v2435) then
											game_ = game

											if game_:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
												DistanceFromCharacter = LocalPlayer_2.DistanceFromCharacter
												Locations = game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations

												if not (DistanceFromCharacter(LocalPlayer_2, Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Carnage").Position) > 1000) then
													continue
												end
											end
										end

										break
									end
								end

								local game_6 = game

								if game_6:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
									local DistanceFromCharacter2 = LocalPlayer_2.DistanceFromCharacter
									local Locations2 = game:GetService("Workspace")._WorldOrigin.Locations

									if not (DistanceFromCharacter2(LocalPlayer_2, Locations2:FindFirstChild("Trial of Carnage").Position) > 1000) then
										continue
									end
								end

								break
							end
						elseif Value == "Cyborg" then
							local game_6

							repeat
								task.wait()
								toTarget(CFrame.new(28282.5703125, 14896.8505859375, 105.10427093505859))
								game_6 = game
							until not game_6:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible
						end
					else

		PlayerTrial = function()
			local Forcefield = workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].FFABorder.Forcefield
			local Position = Forcefield.Position
			local Size = Forcefield.Size
			local v2471 = workspace:FindPartsInRegion3(Region3.new(Position - Size / 2, Position + Size / 2), nil, math.huge)

			for _, v1 in pairs(v2471) do
				local Parent = v1.Parent

				if Parent and Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
					local playerFromCharacter = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Parent)

					if playerFromCharacter and playerFromCharacter.Name ~= LocalPlayer_2.Name and playerFromCharacter.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
						return playerFromCharacter.Character
					end
				end
			end
		end

		hasCooldownChanged = function(p539)
			local attributes2 = p539:GetAttributes()

			if not attributes then
				attributes = p539:GetAttributes()
			end

			for k, v1 in next, attributes2, nil do
				if (string.find(k, "GunCooldown") or string.find(k, "MeleeCooldown") or string.find(k, "SwordCooldown") or string.find(k, "BloxFruitCooldown")) and v1 > 0 and attributes[k] ~= v1 then
					attributes = attributes2

					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end

		GetRainBowHaki = function()
			if game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("HornedMan") == 1 then
				lib.CreateNoti({
					Title = "Banana Cat Hub",
					Desc = "Done Get Rainbow Haki",
					ShowTime = 5
				})
				wait(5)

				return
			end

			local HornedMan = workspace.NPCs:FindFirstChild("Horned Man") or game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").NPCs:FindFirstChild("Horned Man") or NPCManager.getNPCsByName("Horned Man")[1]._modelState._instance

			if DetectQuestRainBowHaki() then
				if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(HornedMan.HumanoidRootPart.Position) > 8 then
					toTarget(HornedMan.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
				else
					wait(2)
					game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("HornedMan", "Bet")
				end
			else
				local v2496 = CheckNameBoss(DetectQuestRainBowHaki(true))

				if v2496 then
					repeat
						task.wait()
						sizepart(v2496)

						if Settings["Select Weapon"] == "Blox Fruit" then
							toTarget(v2496.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-7, 20, 0))
						else
							toTarget(v2496.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(7, 20, 0))
						end

						ClickM1(v2496)
						UsedualFlock()
					until not IsMobAlive(v2496) or not Settings["Auto Get Rainbow Haki"]

					lib.CreateNoti({

						ShowTime = 5

			local n54 = 0
			local v2503

			for _, child in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
				if (typeof(p547) == "table" and table.find(p547, child.Name) or child.Name == p547) and IsMobAlive(child) then
					local Health = child.Humanoid.Health

					if n54 < Health then
						n54 = Health
						v2503 = child
					end
				end
			end

			return v2503
		end

							end

								repeat
									task.wait()
									fireclickdetector(Tablet[k].ClickDetector)
								until string.find(tostring(Tablet[k].Line.Rotation.Z), s16)

								print(k, s16)
							end
						end
					end
				elseif LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(Vector3.new(-8761.4765625, 142.10487365722656, 6086.07861328125)) > 50 then
					toTarget(CFrame.new(-8761.4765625, 142.10487365722656, 6086.07861328125))
				else
					for _, v1 in pairs({
						game.workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Placard1.Right.ClickDetector,
						game.workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Placard2.Right.ClickDetector,
						game.workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Placard3.Left.ClickDetector,
						game.workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Placard4.Right.ClickDetector,
						game.workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Placard5.Left.ClickDetector,
						game.workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Placard6.Left.ClickDetector,
						game.workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Placard7.Left.ClickDetector
					}) do
						fireclickdetector(v1)
					end
				end
			elseif game.Lighting.Sky.MoonTextureId == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9709149431" and (game.Lighting.ClockTime > 16 or game.Lighting.ClockTime < 5) then
				if LocalPlayer_2:DistanceFromCharacter(Vector3.new(-8654.314453125, 140.94990539550781, 6167.5283203125)) > 50 then
					toTarget(CFrame.new(-8654.314453125, 140.94990539550781, 6167.5283203125))
				end

				CommF:InvokeServer("gravestoneEvent", 2)
				CommF:InvokeServer("gravestoneEvent", 2, true)

					Title = "Banana Cat Hub",

				})
